The README specifies the four-byte size header and ring-buffer command stream. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/dragon-soul-aup-viewer/))
- [asm-differ](https://github.com/simonlindholm/asm-differ) — tool · binary, game — Diffs original and rebuilt assembly for one function or ROM address to guide matching decompilation; its watch mode can rerun make when sources change. The README describes it as decomp.me’s default diff viewer and points to objdiff as a modern alternative. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/asm-differ/))
- [Aspyr Archive Tool](https://github.com/Dhamp1r/AspyrArchiveTool) — tool · game — Aspyr Archive Tool unpacks and rebuilds Android OBB archives used by three Aspyr game ports: Star Wars: Knights of the Old Republic II, Jade Empire: Special Edition and Fahrenheit: Indigo Prophecy Remastered. Its command-line options support full or selective compression and a CRC64 checksum for Jade Empire. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/aspyr-archive-tool/))
- [AssetRipper](https://github.com/AssetRipper/AssetRipper) — tool · game — Analyze Unity game files, inspect asset dependencies, and convert supported assets into Unity project formats. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/assetripper/))
- [AssetStudio](https://github.com/Perfare/AssetStudio) — tool · game — Tool for exploring, extracting and exporting the assets and asset bundles of Unity versions 3.4 to 2022.1. It converts Texture2D and Sprite images to PNG, TGA, JPEG or BMP and AudioClip to MP3, OGG, WAV, M4A or FSB, with FSB-to-WAV conversion, and exports fonts, meshes as OBJ, text assets, shaders, movie and video clips and MonoBehaviour data as JSON; models export from the scene hierarchy, and Animators export to FBX with their bound AnimationClips. MonoBehaviour export asks for the folder holding the assemblies, such as Managed, or, for IL2CPP builds, the dummy DLLs that Il2CppDumper generates. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/assetstudio/))
- [BepInEx](https://github.com/BepInEx/BepInEx) — tool · game — Plugin and patching framework for Unity Mono, Unity IL2CPP and .NET Framework games such as XNA, FNA and MonoGame titles, which loads mods and analysis plugins into a locally installed game. Plugins patch methods at runtime with the bundled HarmonyX and MonoMod.RuntimeDetour, while preloader patchers rewrite managed assemblies with Mono.Cecil before the game loads them; on IL2CPP games it runs Cpp2IL and Il2CppInterop to generate interop assemblies from the game binary and global-metadata.dat. Its guides also cover configuration, logging, and debugging plugins and game assemblies with dnSpy. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/bepinex/))
- [BGI Converter TGA](https://github.com/yutaro023/BGI-Ethornell_ConverterTGA_Sekai_VN) — tool · game — BGI Converter TGA analyzes BGI-Ethornell game image files and converts custom RGB or RGBA layouts and BMP images into 32-bit TGA. Its C++17 utility detects formats from headers, reports dimensions and offsets, and supports single-file or batch conversion. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/bgi-ethornell-converter-tga/))
- [BNDExtract](https://github.com/fferradamerino/bnd_extract) — tool · game — BNDExtract is a Go command-line tool for extracting the ALL.BND archive used by Kuon and some other FromSoftware PlayStation 2 games. It takes a .bnd file and an output directory, providing access to the archive's contents for inspection. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/bndextract-kuon/))
- [BSPSource](https://github.com/ata4/bspsrc) — tool · game — Java map decompiler that turns Source engine .bsp map files back into .vmf files that can be loaded in Hammer. It also has an integrated pakfile extractor. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/bspsrc/))
- [Chip-8 Emulator and c8-disassembler](https://github.com/lem2clide/Chip8-Emulator) — tool · game — The c8-disassembler in Chip-8 Emulator translates CHIP-8 ROM opcodes into assembly instructions. Its source and sample Pong ROM listing show the supported output; the repository also contains a companion emulator whose SDL2 display is marked as work in progress. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/chip8-emulator-disassembler/))
- [Cpp2IL](https://github.com/SamboyCoding/Cpp2IL) — tool · app, binary, game — Recover managed representations from Unity IL2CPP builds using binary and metadata analysis; reconstruction remains a work in progress. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/cpp2il/))
- [decomp-permuter](https://github.com/simonlindholm/decomp-permuter) — tool · binary, game — Randomly or systematically permutes one C function, recompiling each variant and scoring it against a target object file to search for matching compiler output. PERM macros specify manual alternatives, and the optional permuter@home mode distributes runs to other workers. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/decomp-permuter/))
- [decomp-toolkit](https://github.com/encounter/decomp-toolkit) — tool · binary, game — GameCube/Wii matching-decompilation toolkit that analyzes DOL and REL executables and, from a project configuration, splits them into relocatable objects plus an ldscript.lcf for CodeWarrior's linker, so decompiled objects can replace original ones while the relinked binary is checked against the original. Analysis covers function boundaries, a Metrowerks/SDK signature database, relocations, sections and data objects, and objects are written without an assembler. Its CLI also handles disc images, archives, compression formats, CodeWarrior demangling and DWARF dumps. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/decomp-toolkit/))
- [decomp.me](https://decomp.me) — tool · binary, game — Collaborative decompilation website for matching work: its compile service builds candidate source with a selected platform compiler, assembles the target assembly and diffs the two objects with asm-differ. m2c drafts initial decompilations for its MIPS, PowerPC, ARM and SuperH platforms. The 18 platform definitions span N64, PlayStation, PS2, PSP, GameCube/Wii, Wii U, DS, GBA, 3DS, Switch, Saturn, Dreamcast and Xbox 360 plus IRIX, Mac OS X, MS-DOS, Win32 and Android x86. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/decomp-me/))
- [dfsdk](https://github.com/3r4y/dfsdk) — tool · game — dfsdk dumps Delta Force object metadata by enumerating its object array, decoding per-entry XOR-encrypted names and walking class super chains and property lists. The README documents name-pool addressing, pointer masking, selected field offsets and example output. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/dfsdk/))
- [DiztinGUIsh](https://github.com/DizTools/DiztinGUIsh) — tool · game — Windows disassembler for Super NES ROMs in which the user marks bytes as code or data, steps through 65816 code while the tool tracks the M and X flags and the data bank and direct page registers, adds labels and comments, and exports assembly for the asar assembler that the README says compiles back into the original binary. Execution context can come from BizHawk or BSNES trace logs, BSNES usage maps and BizHawk code/data logs, or live capture over a socket from a custom bsnes-plus build. Project files keep the annotations in merge-friendly XML with a link and checksum for the ROM rather than the ROM itself, so several people can work on one disassembly. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/diztinguish/))
- [dtk-template](https://github.com/encounter/dtk-template) — tool · game — Project template for starting GameCube and Wii matching-decompilation repositories around decomp-toolkit. Its configure.py generates a ninja build that analyzes and splits the game's DOL and REL files from per-version config.yml, symbols.txt and splits.txt files, compiles the decompiled C/C++ with period Metrowerks compilers, relinks the result and checks it against recorded SHA-1 hashes. It also wires in objdiff for per-object diffing, progress reporting for decomp.dev and an example GitHub Actions workflow. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/dtk-template/))
- [DualRip](https://github.com/TetraSsky/DualRip) — tool · game — DualRip extracts Nintendo DS sound effects and music from SDAT archives and Nintendo 3DS audio from CSAR archives, including ROM inputs. It parses sequence, wave and stream formats, emulates the consoles' sound drivers for rendering and exports WAV files with loop points through CLI and GUI frontends. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/dualrip/))
- [Ed1th's PvZ Toolkit](https://github.com/LambdaEd1th/ed1ths-pvz-toolkit) — tool · game — Ed1th's PvZ Toolkit inspects and edits Plants vs. Zombies resource archives and files through a desktop or web interface backed by Rust format libraries. Its workspaces cover RSB/RSG, PAK and DZip archives, RTON and NEWTON data, PAM animation, Compiled Text and Wwise WEM audio, among other formats. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ed1ths-pvz-toolkit/))
- [ffxii-tza-tools](https://github.com/StyuDEV/ffxii-tza-tools) — tool · game — ffxii-tza-tools documents and processes FINAL FANTASY XII The Zodiac Age character formats, including .ca animations, .cm models and .phyre assets. Its Python tools decode chunk structures, export models and animations to glTF, edit curves and repack @FRP containers from files extracted from the user’s game copy. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ffxii-tza-tools/))
- [FH6 Model Tools](https://github.com/noidex0/fh6-model-tools) — tool · game — FH6 Model Tools parses reverse-engineered Forza Horizon 6 asset formats, including burG model and texture containers. Python converters export car meshes to GLB or OBJ, textures to DDS, and selected material, palette, lighting, and ambient-occlusion data to readable outputs. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/fh6-model-tools/))
- [FModel](https://github.com/4sval/FModel) — tool · game — Explore Unreal Engine game archives with CUE4Parse-backed package and property inspection, asset previews and export. The 2026 summer release adds USD world export for reconstructing supported scene content. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/fmodel/))
- [frida-il2cpp-bridge](https://github.com/vfsfitvnm/frida-il2cpp-bridge) — tool · app, game — Inspect IL2CPP classes, methods, and fields in a live process, and trace or intercept method calls without supplying global-metadata.dat. Complements static IL2CPP dumpers with runtime observation. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/frida-il2cpp-bridge/))
- [GARbro](https://github.com/morkt/GARbro) — tool · game — Browse recognized visual-novel resource archives as folders, preview their contents, and extract images or audio with conversion to common formats. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/garbro/))
- [GB Recompiled](https://github.com/arcanite24/gb-recompiled) — tool · binary, game — Static recompiler that turns Game Boy and Game Boy Color ROMs into portable C projects: it decodes and analyzes banked LR35902 code, emits C with machine-readable metadata, and links it with a shared SDL2 runtime for video, audio, input, saves and hardware timing. Jump targets that static analysis missed fall back to an interpreter. The repository also carries differential, replay and frame and audio capture tooling, plus a Pokémon Crystal port that builds native features on hooks into the exact ROM. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/gb-recompiled/))
- [ghidra-allegrex](https://github.com/kotcrab/ghidra-allegrex) — tool · binary, firmware, game — Ghidra processor module for the Allegrex CPU of the PlayStation Portable, derived from Ghidra's MIPS module. It adds Allegrex-specific instructions, VFPU disassembly and basic decompilation, the PSP calling convention, and an ELF loader that detects PSP executables and applies the PSP-specific type A and B relocation sections, with rebasing after import. Bundled scripts import and export PPSSPP .sym function-label files, and an exporter writes kernel modules as relocatable object files for linking. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ghidra-allegrex/))
- [GM8Decompiler](https://github.com/OpenGMK/GM8Decompiler) — tool · game — Command-line tool that turns games built with GameMaker 8.0 or 8.1 back into editable .gmk or .gm81 project files. It reads the gamedata section that a built executable carries next to the runner and writes its settings, sprites, sounds, backgrounds, paths, scripts, fonts, timelines, objects, rooms, extensions, included files and GML code in the project format matching the game's version. A Rust rewrite on the OpenGMK project's gm8exe library that replaced the team's fork of WastedMeerkat's gm81decompiler, it is now developed in the OpenGMK repository alongside the GM8Emulator runner reimplementation. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/gm8decompiler/))
- [Godot RE Tools](https://github.com/GDRETools/gdsdecomp) — tool · game — Recover supported Godot project files, GDScript and imported resources from exported games. Includes PCK inspection and extraction, script decompilation, and resource conversion for examining and rebuilding project structure. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/godot-re-tools/))
- [GRB Modding Knowledgebase](https://github.com/dataterminals/grb-modding-knowledgebase) — project · game — GRB Modding Knowledgebase documents Ghost Recon: Breakpoint’s Anvil .forge archives, nested .data resources and asset replacement workflow. Its Python inspectors examine MotionCloth files, list resource types, compare forge indexes and diff gameplay database records to explore field layouts. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/grb-modding-knowledgebase/))
- [GTA Mode: reverse-engineering SimCity 3000 Unlimited for a native plugin](https://github.com/DenisSergeevitch/simcity3000-gta-mode) — case-study · binary, game — GTA Mode is a native plugin that adds direct control of spawned citizens and cars to SimCity 3000 Unlimited. It uses findings from Ghidra analysis of the game's binaries, GZCOM plugin loading, in-memory city structures and sprite archives. The C plugin runs in the original game, reads city data from its memory and decodes art from the user's installation; MODLOG.md records addresses, formats and dead ends. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/simcity-3000-gta-mode-case/))
- [ido-static-recomp](https://github.com/decompals/ido-static-recomp) — tool · binary, game — Statically recompiles the IRIX MIPS programs of SGI's IDO 5.3 and 7.1 C toolchain, such as cc, cfe, uopt, ugen and as1, into native Linux, macOS and Windows executables, so matching decompilation projects can build with the original compiler code on modern hosts. It recompiles compilers, not games: it neither decompiles nor translates game code. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ido-static-recomp/))
- [Il2CppDumper](https://github.com/Perfare/Il2CppDumper) — tool · app, binary, game — Recover IL2CPP metadata and dummy assemblies, and generate helper scripts for native analysis tools; method code is not restored. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/il2cppdumper/))
- [Il2CppInspector](https://github.com/djkaty/Il2CppInspector) — tool · app, binary, game — Static analysis tool for Unity IL2CPP applications that reads an IL2CPP binary with its global-metadata.dat and outputs the application's type structure and method addresses as C# stub code, .NET assembly shim DLLs for decompilers such as ILSpy and dnSpy, IDA and Ghidra Python scripts that apply symbols and types, C++ type scaffolding and JSON address maps. Three APIs, also published as a NuGet package, and a plugin SDK expose the binary metadata, .NET type model and C++ model for custom analysis. The README states that it is not a decompiler and does not recover method source code. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/il2cppinspector/))
- [KBot](https://github.com/coreprime/kbot) — tool · game — KBot inspects and transforms Total Annihilation and TA: Kingdoms game files, pairing a Go CLI with a proprietary format reference. It decompiles COB bytecode to BOS source, disassembles scripts, checks round trips and examines formats including HPI archives, GAF sprites, TNT maps and 3DO models. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/kbot/))
- [kenshi-modkit](https://github.com/LucasSKrewer/kenshi-modkit) — tool · game — kenshi-modkit parses and writes Kenshi .mod and .base files and reads its .save, .zone and .platoon files using Python. Its format notes document filetypes 15–17 and inferred typecodes, while utilities dump records, compare saves, detect mod conflicts and check byte-identical round trips. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/kenshi-modkit/))
- [LuaJIT Decompiler v2](https://github.com/marsinator358/luajit-decompiler-v2) — tool · binary, game — Decompiles LuaJIT bytecode files back into Lua source, written as a replacement for the older Python LuaJIT decompiler. The README reports full support for gotos and for stripped bytecode, including reconstructed locals and upvalues, and its boolean-expression recovery follows a published decompilation algorithm. It processes single files or whole folders and writes .lua output. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/luajit-decompiler-v2/))
- [m2c](https://github.com/matt-kempster/m2c) — tool · binary, game — Decompiler for matching projects that turns GNU as-format 32-bit MIPS, ARM, PowerPC and SuperH assembly into draft C, with partial C++ support. A target combines platform, compiler (IDO, GCC or MetroWerks CodeWarrior) and language; context files supply types, and it can infer unknown struct fields and emit global initializers. The README places it alongside splat, asm-differ and decomp-permuter, and decomp.me calls it for initial decompilations. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/m2c/))
- [MelonLoader](https://github.com/LavaGang/MelonLoader) — tool · game — Mod loader for Unity games on both the Mono and IL2CPP runtimes, which loads plugins and mods from folders in a locally installed game; on Windows a proxy DLL, version.dll by default, starts it with the game. For IL2CPP games its assembly generator runs Cpp2IL and Il2CppInterop to produce managed interop assemblies, and HarmonyX and MonoMod are bundled for runtime patching. Its logs can capture Unity player output and Harmony messages, and debug options provide a Mono debug server for Mono games and a wait-for-debugger option for IL2CPP games. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/melonloader/))
- [mgbdis](https://github.com/mattcurrie/mgbdis) — tool · binary, game — Python disassembler that turns a Game Boy or Game Boy Color ROM, including multi-bank ROMs, into RGBDS-compatible assembly plus a Makefile that reassembles it, so the rebuilt ROM can be compared with the input. An optional .sym file beside the ROM supplies labels and marks code, data, text, padding and 1bpp/2bpp tile-image blocks; images are written as PNG files that rgbgfx converts back, text can use custom character maps, and hardware registers get hardware.inc names. The README explains that SM83 instructions vary in length and data is interleaved with code, so marked code blocks keep it from disassembling mid-instruction. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/mgbdis/))
- [N64: Recompiled](https://github.com/N64Recomp/N64Recomp) — tool · binary, game — Statically recompiles N64 MIPS binaries into C functions from symbol metadata, emitting function lookups and relocation macros so a runtime can handle statically linked and relocatable overlays. The output needs a runtime such as N64ModernRuntime; the repository also includes RSP microcode and mod tooling. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/n64recomp/))
- [N64ModernRuntime](https://github.com/N64Recomp/N64ModernRuntime) — tool · game — Runtime for N64Recomp static recompilations and traditional source ports of N64 games, made of two libraries. ultramodern reimplements core libultra services (threads, message queues, timers, controllers, audio, RSP task handling and VI timing) and leaves controller input, audio playback and the graphics renderer, with RT64 recommended, to callbacks the host project registers. librecomp bridges N64Recomp-generated code to ultramodern and adds overlay handling, PI DMA ROM reads, and EEPROM, SRAM and Flash RAM saves. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/n64modernruntime/))
- [Nebula (NebulaFD)](https://github.com/AITYunivers/NebulaFD) — tool · game — Work-in-progress decompiler for games made with Clickteam Fusion 2.5, with fixes for older Multimedia Fusion 1.5 files. Its .NET console app reads Windows executables, Android APKs, iOS IPAs, CCN data files and existing MFA projects, then runs plug-in tools: an MFA Parser that writes the game back out as an editable Fusion project, and an Asset Dumper for images, sounds, music, fonts, icons, shaders and packed files. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/nebulafd/))
- [NTRGhidra](https://github.com/pedro-javierf/NTRGhidra) — tool · game — Ghidra loader and plugin for Nintendo DS programs. It checks the .nds header, maps either the ARM9 or the ARM7 binary at its RAM address together with the console's memory regions and I/O register labels, decompresses SDK-compressed ARM9 code and overlays, and fills the overlays the user selects at import, while an optional overlay-manager plugin swaps overlays in and out afterwards. The repository also keeps a small Ghidra Function ID database of NitroSDK 3.0 routines for function identification. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ntrghidra/))
- [nxnative](https://github.com/seedds/nxnative) — tool · binary, game — nxnative is a tool that packages Nintendo Switch games as apps that run their code natively on Apple hardware, with graphics translated to Metal. It builds iPhone, iPad, or Mac apps from supported Switch games; Switch 2 builds are documented for Mac only. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/nxnative/))
- [objdiff](https://github.com/encounter/objdiff) — tool · binary, game — Compare functions and data in reference and rebuilt object files to guide matching decompilation. Displays instruction-level differences, demangles C++ symbols, and can rebuild when source files change. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/objdiff/))
- [PCSX-Redux](https://github.com/grumpycoders/pcsx-redux) — tool · binary, game — PlayStation 1 emulator forked from PCSX and built around development and reverse engineering, with an interpreter and dynamic recompiler, software and OpenGL renderers, a MIPS debugger, VRAM viewer, SPU and GPU debugging tools, and a built-in GDB server that its documentation connects to gdb, IDA Pro and Ghidra's debugger. A LuaJIT API exposes breakpoints, memory and registers, an assembler and an ISO builder, and an optional REST web server can dump RAM and VRAM. The repository also holds the OpenBIOS replacement BIOS, PS-EXE conversion and packing tools, a Psy-Q object-file converter, Ghidra scripts for exchanging symbols and overlays with the emulator, and PS1 hardware designs. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/pcsx-redux/))
- [pkg-to-anyps5](https://github.com/awake-devel/pkg-to-anyps5) — tool · game — pkg-to-anyps5 extracts PS5 debug game packages or game folders into the layout expected by the AnyPS5 relinker. It parses FIH, PFS and NAPS structures, decodes supported Kraken blocks, and unwraps fake-signed SELF executables and modules into ELF files. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/pkg-to-anyps5/))
- [PKHeX](https://github.com/kwsch/PKHeX) — tool · game — PKHeX is a C# Windows Forms editor for Pokémon core-series save data. It can edit and save supported files, import Pokémon and teams, and convert data between game generations. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/pkhex/))
- [PS2 Web Recomp](https://github.com/snuri00/ps2-web-recomp) — tool · binary, game — Statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games for the browser: eerecomp translates the Emotion Engine code of a game's boot executable into C++ that is compiled to WebAssembly, and a runtime emulates the rest of the console, including a high-level EE kernel, an interpreted I/O processor running the disc's IRX modules, the vector units, SPU2 sound and the Graphics Synthesizer on WebGPU compute shaders. Its port script repeats headless runs to find functions reached through pointers and vector-unit microprograms to compile ahead of time, then builds the web page and a native runner. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ps2-web-recomp/))
- [PS2Recomp](https://github.com/ran-j/PS2Recomp) — tool · binary, game — Experimental static recompiler that translates MIPS R5900 code from PlayStation 2 ELF executables into C++ and links it with a bundled runtime. ps2xAnalyzer or a Ghidra export script produces a per-game TOML of functions, stubs, skips and instruction patches; ps2xRecomp emits a literal instruction-by-instruction translation; ps2xRuntime and ps2xIOP supply guest memory, syscall dispatch, IOP module execution with HLE fallbacks and partial, largely stubbed GS/VU hardware. Output is not decompiled source, and getting a game to run remains per-game porting work. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ps2recomp/))
- [ps3recomp](https://github.com/sp00nznet/ps3recomp) — tool · binary, game — Toolkit and runtime for statically recompiling PlayStation 3 games into native executables. Python tools parse PS3 ELF and PRX files, find functions and firmware imports, and lift PPU and SPU code to C/C++; a project template links that code with a C/C++ runtime providing LV2 system calls, high-level emulation of the system libraries and RSX graphics translation to Direct3D 12, Metal or Vulkan. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ps3recomp/))
- [PSP Web Recomp](https://github.com/snuri00/psp-web-recomp) — project · game, website — Translate PSP MIPS executables into C++ and WebAssembly, backed by a PSP high-level emulation runtime and WebGL2 renderer. Native tracing, frame capture and audio tools support investigating missing behavior during game porting. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/psp-web-recomp/))
- [psp-media-slopkit](https://github.com/TotalKommando/psp-media-toolkit) — tool · game — psp-media-slopkit analyses PSP XMB ICON1.PMF and SND0.AT3 files using format findings derived from retail game samples. Its Python CLI checks media against documented XMB limits, converts video and audio into icon media, and inserts the results into a homebrew EBOOT.PBP. FINDINGS.md records PSMF container fields, H.264 constraints and AT3 playback behaviour. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/psp-media-slopkit/))
- [RE4METoolbox](https://github.com/Dhamp1r/RE4ME-Toolbox) — tool · game — RE4METoolbox provides command-line utilities for examining and modifying Resident Evil 4 Mobile Edition files. RE4MEAcvTool unpacks and repacks ACV .bin archives, while RE4METextTool converts Mission and Resource .bin text files to TSV and repacks edited text. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/re4me-toolbox/))
- [REDscript](https://github.com/jac3km4/redscript) — tool · game — Compiler and decompiler toolkit for REDscript, a statically typed, object-oriented language for writing scripts for REDengine, CD Projekt's engine used in Cyberpunk 2077. Its Rust crates parse and analyze source, generate bytecode with monomorphization of generics, decompile .redscripts bytecode bundles back into REDscript source, read and write REDengine's native binary format, and provide a drop-in replacement for CD Projekt's scc script compiler plus a C API with source mapping. The CLI exposes decompile, compile, lint and format commands; the language server and debugger live in a separate redscript-ide repository. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/redscript/))
- [RenderDoc](https://github.com/baldurk/renderdoc) — tool · app, game — Inspect frame captures, draw calls, shaders, and graphics resources in your own game or graphics application. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/renderdoc/))
- [ReXGlue SDK](https://github.com/rexglue/rexglue-sdk) — tool · binary, game — Experimental SDK for statically recompiling Xbox 360 executables into portable C++23 that runs natively on Windows, Linux and macOS. Its rexglue CLI scaffolds a per-game CMake project and, from a TOML config, analyzes a XEX binary (import resolution, PDATA, block discovery, gap filling, jump resolution and call-target validation) before translating each PowerPC instruction into C++; a runtime derived from Xenia's emulator core, with D3D12 or Vulkan graphics, hosts the generated code. Generated functions are weak aliases that native C++ overrides or mid-function hooks can replace, so a port can be refined one function at a time. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/rexglue-sdk/))
- [RtG-Format](https://github.com/JuanCrakYT/RtG-Format) — documentation · game — RtG-Format documents the observed save and construction format of the Roblox game Road To Gramby's. Its references and experiments cover build structures, part identifiers, connections, properties, encoding and compression, with supporting CLI and browser preview tools. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/rtg-format/))
- [ScriptSource-Dumper](https://github.com/uhhKnife/ScriptSource-Dumper) — tool · game — ScriptSource-Dumper extracts scripts from Call of Duty: Black Ops II scriptsrc.clump archives. Its C++ parser reads the archive table of contents and data chunks, locates the internal name table, and writes files under their recovered names and directories. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/scriptsource-dumper/))
- [sims-package2glb](https://github.com/infinition/sims-package2glb) — tool · game — sims-package2glb extracts models from The Sims 2, 3 and 4 .package files into glTF binary files. Its Rust parsers handle DBPF containers, RefPack compression, RCOL and GMDC geometry, textures, materials and recolours. The documentation records format findings including Sims 4 DST texture planes and relative RCOL chunk references. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/sims-package2glb/))
- [Spice86](https://github.com/OpenRakis/Spice86) — tool · binary, game — PC emulator for executing, reverse engineering and rewriting real-mode DOS programs whose source code is unavailable. While a program runs it builds a control-flow graph, and on exit it dumps memory, execution flow, Ghidra-compatible symbols and a self-contained, runnable C# project whose generated functions override the original assembly, so analysts can rewrite them function by function into readable code while the program keeps working. It also provides a GDB remote server with custom analysis commands, a built-in debugger with a C-header structure viewer, an optional HTTP memory API and an MCP server whose tools cover CPU, memory, breakpoints, CFG and device state; an optional Ghidra plugin imports the dumps. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/spice86/))
- [splat](https://github.com/ethteck/splat) — tool · binary, game — Binary splitter for decompilation and modding projects: a YAML config says where and how to cut an N64 ROM or PSX, PS2 or PSP binary into code, data and asset segments. A scan phase disassembles code and data, with MIPS handled by spimdisasm; a split phase writes the files and a linker script for rebuilding the input. Custom segment types can extend the pipeline. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/splat/))
- [swe1r-assets-unity: Racer model-block editor sample](https://github.com/akopetsch/swe1r-assets-unity) — tool · game — Work-in-progress Unity editor sample for importing and modifying Star Wars Episode I: Racer model blocks, then serializing them back through swe1r-assets. Its editor includes an unmodified import/export byte-comparison command; it is an asset-format workflow rather than a reimplementation of the game. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/swe1r-assets-unity/))
- [UABEA](https://github.com/nesrak1/UABEA) — tool · game — Cross-platform reader and writer for the asset bundles and serialized asset files of Unity games, originally based on, though not a fork of, the archived UABE. Its README presents it as a modding and research tool rather than an extractor and points users who only want to extract assets to AssetRipper or AssetStudio; it opens bundles from Unity's Addressables system, whose CRC checks must be cleared with a separate tool before edited bundles load. It builds on AssetsTools.NET with Cpp2IL and Mono.Cecil, and the repository includes texture, audio-clip, font and text-asset plugins. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/uabea/))
- [UE Explorer](https://github.com/UE-Explorer/UE-Explorer) — tool · binary, game — Explore Unreal packages through class, property, and dependency trees; decompile and export UnrealScript classes, inspect bytecode, and examine structured binary data. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ue-explorer/))
- [UE4SS](https://github.com/UE4SS-RE/RE-UE4SS) — tool · game — Inspect Unreal Engine games at runtime through live UObject property views, reflected C++ header dumps, property mappings and actor dumps. Lua and C++ APIs support experiments while reconstructing game structures. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ue4ss/))
- [UELib (Unreal-Library)](https://github.com/EliotVU/Unreal-Library) — tool · game — C# library that reads Unreal Engine 1, 2 and 3 package files such as .u, .upk and .udk, auto-detects a matching game build and deserializes their exports, imports and properties. Its main goal is decompiling UnrealScript bytecode back to source by reconstructing classes, states, functions, structs, enums and default properties; it also deserializes fonts, sounds, palettes and textures. The README’s per-game compatibility table runs from Unreal and Deus Ex to Splinter Cell, Mass Effect and the Batman: Arkham games, and the separate UE Explorer GUI is built on it. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/uelib-unreal-package-library/))
- [UndertaleModTool](https://github.com/UnderminersTeam/UndertaleModTool) — tool · game — Browse and modify supported GameMaker data files, extract assets, and decompile or compile GML VM code. Includes room editing, scripted changes, a CLI, and the reusable UndertaleModLib library. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/undertalemodtool/))
- [Universal GBA Pokedex](https://github.com/TheJazz123/Universal-GBA-Pokedex) — tool · game — Universal GBA Pokedex scans Game Boy Advance Pokémon ROMs to locate and display evolution, name and item tables. It searches byte signatures, scores candidate tables using text and pointer patterns, and aligns entries against known anchors instead of requiring fixed offsets. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/universal-gba-pokedex/))
- [Unrpyc](https://github.com/CensoredUsername/unrpyc) — tool · game — Unrpyc is a tool that decompiles compiled Ren'Py game scripts into readable script files. It runs from the command line or through files placed in a game's directory; it does not extract RPA archives. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/unrpyc/))
- [vc64_240p](https://github.com/kreycai/vc64_240p) — tool · binary, game — vc64_240p analyses and patches the N64 Virtual Console emulator inside Wii WAD channels to output 240p. It finds render-mode structures by pattern, changes video fields and disables deflicker; an optional patch removes the emulator's dark filter. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/vc64-240p/))
- [VG Reverse Engineering](https://github.com/TurnaboutHero/vainglory-replay-decoder) — tool · game — VG Reverse Engineering decodes Vainglory: Community Edition replay files into recorded player names, heroes, K/D/A, items, gold and scoreboard counters. Its offline tools also export JSON and CSV, maintain a SQLite catalog and document VGR record and event interpretation. Output records provenance and leaves unsupported values unresolved. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/vainglory-replay-decoder/))
- [vgmstream](https://github.com/vgmstream/vgmstream) — tool · game — Library and tools that decode streamed, prerecorded video game audio in hundreds of formats and codecs, from common game engine files to single-game codecs, including looped music, subsongs, companion files, custom containers and encryption keys. TXTH text descriptors add support for further formats, including raw audio, and TXTP files set per-file options such as forced looping or merging files. It ships as the vgmstream-cli decoder, which converts supported files to WAV, the vgmstream123 player and plugins for Winamp, foobar2000, XMPlay and Audacious, and an online web player is available at vgmstream.org. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/vgmstream/))
- [xboxrecomp](https://github.com/sp00nznet/xboxrecomp) — tool · binary, game — Static-recompilation toolkit for original Xbox games: a Python pipeline parses a retail XBE, disassembles it, classifies library functions, recovers calling conventions and lifts every x86 function to C. The generated code is compiled in a separate game project that links the toolkit's runtime libraries, which stand in for the Xbox kernel and translate Direct3D 8, NV2A, audio and input to Direct3D 11 and Win32 APIs, or to SDL2 and OpenGL on Linux and macOS. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/xboxrecomp/))
- [XenonRecomp](https://github.com/hedge-dev/XenonRecomp) — tool · binary, game — Converts Xbox 360 PowerPC executables into C++ that can be compiled for other platforms, with the XenonAnalyse companion detecting jump tables and function boundaries. Output is a literal instruction translation rather than decompiled code and needs a runtime that the project does not supply. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/xenonrecomp/))
- [Xenosaga I Decomp Compatibility Kit](https://github.com/LinuxJessi/decomp-compat-kit) — tool · binary, game — Checks a PS2 matching decompilation from outside its own build: the C is recompiled with a stock, unpatched ee-gcc 2.96 and assembler, then each function is byte-compared against the original and every difference is classified by its likely toolchain cause. Companion scripts export ELF symbol tables to CSV and convert them to splat symbol files. Defaults target Xenosaga Episode I; the README says the method generalises but the defaults do not. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/decomp-compat-kit/))
- [Xenus DT1 Decompiler](https://github.com/CodeNoob53/xenus-dt1-decompiler) — tool · game — Xenus DT1 Decompiler extracts DT1 and DT2 texture caches from Xenus 2: White Gold and Boiling Point: Road to Hell; its current decoder has not been tested with The Precursors. Its C# decoder reimplements behavior recovered from VELoader.dll, handles zlib and UCL NRV2E streams, and exports DDS or converted image formats. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/xenus-dt1-decompiler/))
- [YGO-PoC Toolset](https://github.com/bitemydusto/YGO-PoC-Toolset) — tool · game — YGO-PoC Toolset handles resource and save formats from Yu-Gi-Oh! Power of Chaos – Joey the Passion. Its unpacker extracts compressed game resource files such as data.dat, while PoCTools decrypts, edits and re-encrypts system.dat saves; the repository also includes a card editor and DLL mod loader. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ygo-poc-toolset/))
- [Zygisk-Il2CppDumper](https://github.com/Perfare/Zygisk-Il2CppDumper) — tool · app, game — Zygisk module by Perfare, the Il2CppDumper author, that dumps type metadata from inside a Unity IL2CPP Android app while it runs. Built for one package name, it waits for the app to load libil2cpp.so, resolves the runtime's il2cpp API exports, walks the classes of every loaded assembly and writes dump.cs to the app's files directory, listing namespaces, classes, interfaces and enums with field offsets, properties and method signatures annotated with RVA and virtual addresses. On x86 devices running an ARM build of the app, it loads its ARM library through the Houdini native bridge. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/zygisk-il2cppdumper/))

<a id="web-analysis"></a>
## Website analysis tools

- [box.js](https://github.com/CapacitorSet/box-js) — tool · website — Node.js utility for studying malicious JavaScript that runs a sample inside an emulated Windows JScript environment, with WScript, ActiveX, filesystem, registry, WMI and HTTP objects emulated and source rewriting that exposes eval calls and conditional-compilation code. Each analysis writes a results folder with the URLs requested and those that led to an executable payload, every code snippet executed (JavaScript, cmd.exe and PowerShell), files written with their types and hashes, and a list of IOCs; by default downloads are answered with simulated 404 errors so that fallback sites are revealed. Batch runs, an optional prepended file of browser-object stubs, a Docker image, a REST API and export to VirusTotal or Cuckoo support triage at scale. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/box-js/))
- [breakinggood](https://github.com/eklavya2201/breakinggood) — tool · website — breakinggood captures a website's screenshots, cleaned DOM, computed design values, images, and XHR/fetch responses, then generates a Vite, React, and Tailwind rebuild with a mock API replaying observed JSON. Its CLI and MCP server expose site capture alongside a separate Git repository setup workflow. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/breakinggood/))
- [Browser Reverse Engineering Toolkit](https://github.com/Dvurechensky/Browser.Reverse.Engineering.Toolkit) — tool · website — Capture browser-session evidence, crawl websites in a persistent Chromium session, and generate offline architecture/API reports plus OpenAPI, SDK and mock-server drafts from saved captures. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/browser-reverse-engineering-toolkit/))
- [Chrome extension source viewer (CRX Viewer)](https://github.com/Rob--W/crxviewer) — tool · website — Browser extension and web page by Rob Wu for inspecting packaged browser extensions without installing them. On Chrome Web Store, Opera, Firefox, Thunderbird and Microsoft Edge add-on pages it adds a button that downloads the package as a zip or opens it in a viewer, and links to extension packages hosted elsewhere can be opened as well. The viewer reads CRX, NEX, XPI and ZIP files: it lists and filters files, searches content by string or regular expression, beautifies and syntax-highlights code, previews images, opens nested zips, shows per-file hashes and derives the extension ID from the package key, signature or manifest. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/crxviewer/))
- [debundle](https://github.com/1egoman/debundle) — tool · website — Node.js command-line tool that unpacks JavaScript bundles produced by Browserify or Webpack back into a tree of module files, so a bundle can be read file by file instead of as one script. A JSON configuration gives the bundle type, the entry-point module and optional knownPaths that map numeric module IDs to output files or node_modules packages, and it can rewrite a minifier's renamed require identifier inline or through a variable so the output can be rebundled or run in Node. The README says it was built to learn how bundles are structured for a reverse-engineering project and that debundling is lossy: package.json settings and Webpack module names are not recovered, and minified code stays minified. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/debundle/))
- [decode-js](https://github.com/echo094/decode-js) — tool · website — Babel-based AST processor that turns obfuscated JavaScript back into readable source, with one target selected per run: javascript-obfuscator (obfuscator.io), JS-Confuser 2.x up to its high preset, sojson and its v7 variant for jsjiami.com.v7, sojson's jjencode variant, and a generic pass for unreachable code, nested blocks and constant expressions. A second, version-aware obfuscator.io target reports the encoder era it detects and returns nothing, with a reason, rather than a half-decoded program when a string-array layer cannot be read. The JS-Confuser notes list each transform it reverses and those, such as variable renaming, that destroy information and cannot be recovered. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/decode-js/))
- [Dembrandt](https://github.com/dembrandt/dembrandt) — tool · website — Node.js CLI that renders a website in Playwright-driven Chromium or Firefox and reads computed styles from the DOM to extract its design system: logo, color roles with the evidence behind each pick, typography, spacing, borders, shadows, motion, breakpoints and component styles such as buttons and inputs. Results export as W3C design tokens (DTCG), Tailwind v4 or shadcn/ui themes, DESIGN.md, an HTML report or a brand-guide PDF, alongside WCAG contrast checks on real DOM pairs and a CI drift gate against a committed baseline. It also runs as an MCP server for agent clients. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/dembrandt/))
- [DevLens Ultra](https://github.com/Arsh-pixel-cmd/DevLens) — tool · website — DevLens Ultra is a Chrome extension for inspecting and extracting website component structure. It analyzes selected DOM elements, computed styles and framework state such as React Fiber props, then generates React JSX and Tailwind classes. An optional AI step refines selected snippets through a configured provider. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/devlens-ultra/))
- [eRepublik API Documentation](https://github.com/driversti/erepublik-api) — documentation · game, website — eRepublik API Documentation maps endpoints observed in the browser-based strategy game's web client. Its reference pages cover authentication cookies and requests for travel, battles, economy, social features and other game functions, with HTTP methods and example parameters. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/erepublik-api-documentation/))
- [Facebook BitMap Codec](https://github.com/NiceDayZc/FacebookCodec) — tool · website — Facebook BitMap Codec encodes and decodes Facebook web API parameter values using the project's reverse-engineered bitmap, run-length and custom Base64 scheme. Its Python module exposes a FacebookCodec class for converting ID lists to encoded strings and back across parameters including __dyn and __csr. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/facebookcodec/))
- [Garmin Strength Workout API](https://github.com/n1t3k/garmin-strength-api) — documentation · protocol, website — Garmin Strength Workout API documents payloads for creating strength workouts through Garmin Connect's undocumented API. Its reference describes repeat groups, exercise and rest steps, conditions, weights and exercise identifiers, with JSON examples plus a small builder and validator. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/garmin-strength-workout-api/))
- [go-fAST](https://github.com/T14Raptor/go-fAST) — tool · website — Go library for parsing, traversing, transforming and regenerating JavaScript abstract syntax trees, which its README presents for code analysis, obfuscation and deobfuscation. Its parser builds on goja's parsing code, a generated SWC-style visitor API supports depth-first rewriting and cloning of nodes, a resolver tracks scopes and bindings, the ext package adds node-removal, side-effect and value-inference helpers, and a generator prints JavaScript from the tree. The documentation calls it a lightweight, faster alternative to Babel that deliberately omits parent, sibling and reference maps and automated mutation helpers. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/go-fast-js-ast/))
- [har-to-openapi](https://github.com/jonluca/har-to-openapi) — tool · website — TypeScript library and CLI that infers OpenAPI 3.0 or 3.1 documents from HAR captures of HTTP traffic, loosely based on har2openapi. Requests are grouped into one document per domain, with options to turn IDs in paths into parameters, infer query, form and JSON body schemas from observed values, and merge several captures into the same operations. Optional settings extract reusable component schemas, keep multiple redacted examples, mark fields required only when every sample contains them, attach per-field observation counts as evidence and apply an OpenAPI Overlay so hand-written names and corrections survive regeneration. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/har-to-openapi/))
- [Hetty](https://github.com/dstotijn/hetty) — tool · website — Open-source HTTP toolkit for security research, which its author aims to make an alternative to Burp Suite Pro. A single Go binary runs a machine-in-the-middle HTTP proxy backed by its own generated root CA, logs proxied requests and responses in per-project databases with search filters and scope rules, and serves a web admin interface. From that interface an analyst can hold requests and responses for manual review, then edit, forward or cancel them, or copy logged requests into a Sender module to edit and resend them. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/hetty/))
- [HTTP Toolkit](https://github.com/httptoolkit/httptoolkit) — tool · app, protocol, website — Open-source HTTP(S) debugging proxy for Windows, Linux and macOS that sets up interception for browsers, terminals, backend runtimes, Electron apps and Android devices, then shows the full headers and body of every request and response with body decoding, inline header documentation and filtering. Its transparent proxy handles plain HTTP, HTTPS, HTTP/2 and WebSockets on one port; breakpoints rewrite live requests or responses, and a built-in client sends custom requests. This repository is the project's umbrella README and issue tracker, while the UI, server, Mockttp proxy library, Android app and desktop shell live in sibling repositories. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/http-toolkit/))
- [Javascript Deobfuscator](https://github.com/willnode/deobfuscator) — tool · website — Browser-based workbench for deobfuscating JavaScript by hand, also hosted on GitHub Pages, that pairs a Monaco editor with step-by-step rewriting and evaluation commands instead of a one-click pipeline. Formatting commands beautify the code and, on a selection or the whole file, decode escaped strings, convert hex numbers, turn bracketed property access into dot notation and rename _0x-style identifiers to short names; evaluation commands push declarations onto a code stack, evaluate a selected expression against it and substitute JSON results, replace captured variables automatically and collapse chains of aliases. The README says the process is not automatic and expects the analyst to know JavaScript. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/willnode-deobfuscator/))
- [javascript-deobfuscator](https://github.com/ben-sb/javascript-deobfuscator) — tool · website — General-purpose JavaScript deobfuscator for common obfuscation patterns, published on npm as js-deobfuscator and usable as a library or CLI. It unpacks arrays of literals and replaces references to them, removes simple, array and arithmetic proxy functions, simplifies arithmetic and string concatenation, removes dead branches, turns computed member access into static properties, renames hexadecimal identifiers such as _0xca830a and beautifies the result. The author also hosts an online version and maintains a separate deobfuscator for Obfuscator.io output. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ben-sb-javascript-deobfuscator/))
- [javascript-deobfuscator (LostMyCode)](https://github.com/LostMyCode/javascript-deobfuscator) — tool · website — Browser-based Vue app that restores the strings Obfuscator.io hides behind string-array lookups in JavaScript. The user pastes the obfuscated script and names its string array or decoder function, such as _0x439c; the tool classifies the calls as plain array indexing, a one-argument decoder or a two-argument RC4-style decoder, runs the script's own decoder to replace each call with its string literal, rewrites bracketed property access as dot notation and beautifies the result. The author hosts a demo at deo.sigr.io. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/lostmycode-javascript-deobfuscator/))
- [JPEXS Free Flash Decompiler (FFDec)](https://github.com/jindrapetrik/jpexs-decompiler) — tool · game, website — Java desktop decompiler and editor for Adobe Flash SWF files. It decompiles ActionScript 1/2 and AS3 side by side with the P-code they came from, edits scripts as source (experimental) or through the assembler view, and exports or replaces images, shapes, sounds, video, fonts and texts, with SWF-to-FLA conversion, SWF-to-XML round trips and SVG or HTML5 Canvas output. It also opens SWC and ABC files, Scaleform GFx and 64-bit Iggy files, has an ActionScript debugger and deobfuscation passes for obfuscated AS3, and offers its parser and decompiler as the ffdec_lib Java library and a command line. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/jpexs-decompiler/))
- [JS-Deobfuscator (0x1Avram)](https://github.com/0x1Avram/js-deobfuscator) — tool · website — Node.js deobfuscator for JavaScript produced by Timofey Kachalov's javascript-obfuscator, also offered as obfuscator.io. It parses the sample with esprima and runs eleven stages named after the obfuscator's own, which remove injected self-defending and debug-protection code, unwind control-flow flattening and dead-code injection, restore converted literals and object keys, replace string-array calls with the decoded strings, simplify the result and recursively process code passed to eval. String-array code is detected automatically or supplied as separate files, and an Examples folder pairs original, obfuscated and deobfuscated samples. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/0x1avram-js-deobfuscator/))
- [jsdeob-workbench](https://github.com/Owl4444/jsdeob-workbench) — tool · website — Inspect JavaScript ASTs and variable scopes while building visual chains of Babel transforms. Save analysis projects and reusable plugins to iteratively simplify obfuscated scripts. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/jsdeob-workbench/))
- [jsluice](https://github.com/BishopFox/jsluice) — tool · website — Bishop Fox's Go library and command-line tool that parses JavaScript with tree-sitter to recover the endpoints a script calls, judging values by how the code uses them rather than by regular expressions alone. URL extraction follows location assignments, window.open, fetch, XMLHttpRequest and jQuery request calls, keeps the HTTP method, headers and query or body parameters, and stands in a placeholder for values only known at run time. Other modes flag credential-shaped values, print syntax trees, run tree-sitter queries and reformat minified code. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/jsluice/))
- [JStillery](https://github.com/mindedsecurity/JStillery) — tool · website — Minded Security's JavaScript deobfuscator, which Stefano Di Paola forked from esdeobfuscate in 2015, simplifies scripts by partial evaluation on an esprima AST. It folds constant and string expressions, propagates known values through variables and pure function calls, evaluates calls to built-in String, Array and Number methods and atob/btoa, and parses string arguments passed to eval back into code. It runs as a command-line tool on a file or stdin, as an Express server with a web UI and a REST endpoint, or as a browserified library. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/jstillery/))
- [minusone](https://github.com/airbus-cert/minusone) — tool · website — Airbus CERT's Rust deobfuscation engine for scripts: it parses PowerShell or JavaScript with tree-sitter, applies rules that infer the values of syntax-tree nodes and prints a simplified script. The PowerShell rules fold arithmetic, string operations, casts, -join, -replace and format operators, Base64 and UTF decoding, predictable variables and loops, and also resolve Gzip or Deflate streams and AES decryption when the data and key are in the script; the newer JavaScript rules cover literals, operators, string and array built-ins, atob and btoa, Buffer calls, predictable function calls and JSFuck constructs. The CLI can list, select or skip rules and export every rewrite step as HTML or JSON, and Python and WebAssembly bindings embed the engine, including in an online version linked from the README. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/minusone-script-deobfuscator/))
- [Mirrorframe](https://github.com/byte271/mirrorframe) — tool · website — Mirrorframe captures webpages with Playwright and reconstructs their observed layout and behavior as a self-contained React app. It records computed styles, assets, motion and selected interaction states, then compares rendered nodes and replayed states against the original with image diffs. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/mirrorframe/))
- [mitmproxy](https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy) — tool · app, protocol, website — Inspect and record HTTP traffic with an interactive proxy, web interface, or scriptable command-line tools. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/mitmproxy/))
- [mitmproxy2swagger](https://github.com/alufers/mitmproxy2swagger) — tool · protocol, website — Command-line tool that reverse-engineers undocumented REST APIs from captured traffic. It reads a mitmproxy flow file, or a HAR export from the browser DevTools, and generates an OpenAPI 3.0 specification describing the observed endpoints. Running it again over further captures safely merges the new data into an existing schema. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/mitmproxy2swagger/))
- [ngrev](https://github.com/mgechev/ngrev) — tool · website — Electron desktop application that statically analyzes an Angular project's TypeScript sources and draws interactive graphs of its NgModules, components, directives, pipes and providers and how they depend on each other, without running the application. A project is loaded through its tsconfig file; views move from the module tree to individual modules, templates and providers, with fuzzy symbol search and PNG export of the current graph. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ngrev/))
- [Obfuscator.io Deobfuscator](https://github.com/ben-sb/obfuscator-io-deobfuscator) — tool · website — Deobfuscator dedicated to JavaScript produced by Obfuscator.io (javascript-obfuscator), which its README says also handles most forks. Working statically on a Babel AST, it reveals strings hidden in rotated, Base64- or RC4-encoded string arrays, inlines proxy functions, simplifies objects, arithmetic and string concatenation, removes dead code and the obfuscator's anti-tamper wrappers, and reverses control-flow flattening. Its configuration is detected automatically, and the author also hosts an online version. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/obfuscator-io-deobfuscator/))
- [REstringer](https://github.com/HumanSecurity/restringer) — tool · website — HUMAN Security's Node.js JavaScript deobfuscator. It identifies the obfuscation type, runs matching processors for obfuscator.io, Caesar Plus and array-based patterns, then iterates 40+ AST modules that rebuild strings, resolve expressions and calls and simplify control flow; unsafe modules evaluate code in a sandbox. It works as a CLI or library. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/restringer/))
- [screenshot-to-code](https://github.com/abi/screenshot-to-code) — tool · website — Generate HTML/CSS, Tailwind, React, Vue, Bootstrap or Ionic front-end code from screenshots, mockups, Figma designs or screen recordings with multimodal models; an optional headless-browser preview lets the model check its rendered output. The result is a visual reconstruction of the interface, not recovered original source or backend behavior. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/screenshot-to-code/))
- [shuji](https://github.com/paazmaya/shuji) — tool · website — Command-line tool that writes the original JavaScript and CSS sources embedded in source maps back out as files. It reads .map files, or JavaScript and CSS files whose sourceMappingURL holds an inline Base64 or URL-encoded map or points to a map in the same folder, and can walk directories with a regular-expression filter. Output is flattened to file names by default, or keeps the map's folder structure with --preserve. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/shuji/))
- [sourcemapper](https://github.com/denandz/sourcemapper) — tool · website — Go command-line tool that recreates a web front end's original JavaScript source tree from a source map generated by webpack or a similar bundler, writing each embedded source file under the path the map records. It takes a .map file by URL or local path, walks a directory of .map files, or fetches a JavaScript file and follows its sourceMappingURL comment or SourceMap response header, including inline data: maps. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/sourcemapper/))
- [Spectral](https://github.com/spectral-mcp/spectral) — integration · app, protocol, website — Analyze captured application HTTP traffic with an LLM to generate MCP tools, OpenAPI 3.1 specifications, or GraphQL schemas. Generated tools call the observed API directly after capture and authentication setup. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/spectral-mcp/))
- [spy-debugger](https://github.com/wuchangming/spy-debugger) — tool · app, website — spy-debugger is a tool for remotely debugging pages in mobile browsers and app WebViews while capturing HTTP and HTTPS traffic. It injects weinre debugging code into HTML responses and can use its built-in proxy or an external one. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/spy-debugger/))
- [Substack API Reference (Unofficial)](https://github.com/AnthonyDavidAdams/substack-api-reference) — documentation · protocol, website — Substack API Reference documents the web app's undocumented JSON API using curl probes and captured browser requests. It records read and write endpoints, authentication and request bodies, and supplies an OpenAPI 3.1 specification with curl and TypeScript examples. The author reports 129 verified endpoints from 14 capture rounds. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/substack-api-reference/))
- [synchrony](https://github.com/relative/synchrony) — tool · website — JavaScript cleaner and deobfuscator aimed mainly at output from javascript-obfuscator (obfuscator.io). It parses a script with acorn and runs a fixed pipeline of AST transformers that fold expressions, clean member accesses, remove dead branches, split sequence expressions, undo switch-based control-flow flattening and decode string arrays, re-implementing their base64 and RC4 decoders in TypeScript rather than executing the input. It ships on npm as deobfuscator with a synchrony CLI and a library API, and optional renaming replaces identifiers with generated word names rather than recovering the originals. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/synchrony/))
- [Thug](https://github.com/buffer/thug) — tool · website — Thug is a Python low-interaction honeyclient that mimics a web browser to detect and emulate malicious content. It supports the study of client-side attacks. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/thug/))
- [unwebpack-sourcemap](https://github.com/rarecoil/unwebpack-sourcemap) — tool · website — unwebpack-sourcemap is a Python script that recovers TypeScript, JSX, and other source files from Webpack source maps. It accepts local or remote source maps and can search a web page's scripts for them. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/unwebpack-sourcemap/))
- [Wakaru](https://github.com/pionxzh/wakaru) — tool · website — Recover readable modules from shipped JavaScript bundles by reversing recognized minifier and transpiler patterns. Supports bundle splitting and modern-syntax recovery for inspecting production frontend code. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/wakaru/))
- [web-crawler ReverseAgent](https://github.com/xiaomaozjj666/web-crawler) — tool · website — web-crawler includes ReverseAgent for investigating website JavaScript and request signing. It hooks fetch, XHR, cookies, crypto.subtle and webpack activity in a browser, captures traffic, separates webpack modules and uses an LLM to reimplement a discovered signature algorithm in Python. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/web-crawler-reverse-agent/))
- [Wirebrowser](https://github.com/fcavallarin/wirebrowser) — tool · website — Wirebrowser is a desktop tool for instrumenting browser JavaScript through the Chrome DevTools Protocol. It can hook functions, trace where values originate, search and modify runtime objects, and intercept or replay HTTP traffic. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/wirebrowser/))

<a id="protocol-analysis"></a>
## Protocol analysis tools

- [AALpy](https://github.com/DES-Lab/AALpy) — tool · protocol — General-purpose Python automata-learning library that infers deterministic, non-deterministic, stochastic and pushdown models of black-box systems from active queries or passive traces. Its README links a separate Bluetooth Low Energy study whose repository reports learned connection and legacy-pairing state machines; message formats are not inferred. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/aalpy/))
- [AC4 docs](https://github.com/Phoenix-Assassins/ac4-docs) — documentation · game, protocol — AC4 docs records online service interfaces for the PC version of Assassin's Creed IV Black Flag. Its RMC documents list method IDs and request and response types, while the repository also contains HTTP API specifications for service revival work. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ac4-docs/))
- [Apple Continuity Reverse Engineering Toolkit](https://github.com/seemoo-lab/apple-continuity-tools) — tool · protocol — Three macOS helpers from SEEMOO Lab that the Open Wireless Link project used to study Apple wireless services such as AirDrop, Wi-Fi Password Sharing, Handoff and Offline Finding from the host side. process_recon summarizes which processes, log categories and subsystems emit log entries matching chosen keywords; keychain_access uses Frida to hook SecItemCopyMatching in a process and exports the keychain items it accesses to JSON; and lldb breakpoint commands print the Handoff and Universal Clipboard messages that the rapportd daemon sends and receives. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/apple-continuity-tools/))
- [Assassin's Creed III online service docs](https://github.com/Phoenix-Assassins/ac3-docs) — documentation · game, protocol — Assassin's Creed III online service docs record method IDs and request and response fields for the PC game's online services. The files cover RMC services such as online saves and account management, alongside notes on P2P Duplicated Objects over PRUDP. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ac3-online-service-docs/))
- [Automated CAN Payload Reverse Engineering](https://github.com/brent-stone/CAN_Reverse_Engineering) — tool · protocol — Research Python and R scripts that automate reverse engineering of Controller Area Network (CAN) payloads recorded from passenger vehicles, implementing the pipeline from Brent Stone's AFIT dissertation. The Python pipeline pre-processes a CAN log into per-arbitration-ID data frames, pulls out public SAE J1979 diagnostic data, runs a lexical analysis that guesses individual signal time-series boundaries, then a semantic analysis that builds a correlation matrix and hierarchically clusters related signals, with plots and a dendrogram as output. A separate R script set applies the rEDM empirical dynamic modeling package to engine-RPM, brake-pressure and vehicle-speed time series to study causal relationships. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/can-reverse-engineering/))
- [Blackbox Protobuf](https://github.com/nccgroup/blackboxprotobuf) — tool · app, protocol — NCC Group's tools for decoding and re-encoding Protocol Buffers messages without the matching .proto definition. The decoder returns the message data with an editable type definition built from best-effort guesses based on wire types; analysts can name fields, correct types and re-encode edited messages, which protoc --decode_raw cannot do. It ships as a Python library with a bbpb CLI, a Jython Burp Suite extension and a mitmproxy addon, and its README traces it to modifying messages during mobile penetration tests. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/blackbox-protobuf/))
- [BtleJuice](https://github.com/DigitalSecurity/btlejuice) — tool · protocol — BtleJuice is a framework for intercepting Bluetooth Low Energy GATT operations between devices. Its web interface displays intercepted operations and supports replay, data modification, and export. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/btlejuice/))
- [Cabana (standalone openpilot-cabana)](https://github.com/deanlee/openpilot-cabana) — tool · protocol — Qt desktop CAN and CAN FD analyzer for decoding signals and reverse engineering unknown messages, split out of openpilot's cabana by Dean Lee, a frequent contributor to it, and now maintained on its own. It shows live per-bit change heatmaps, colors bytes by trend such as counters, toggles or noisy data, searches for signals by value or for bits that change together across messages and buses, mutes known bits, plots signals and edits DBC files with undo, while replaying openpilot drives with synchronized camera video. Sources are openpilot routes, Vector ASC, candump and PEAK TRC logs, SocketCAN, comma.ai Panda devices and live streams from a comma device, with a bundled set of opendbc DBC files. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/openpilot-cabana/))
- [CAN Bus Parser](https://github.com/Knight1/vanmoof-canbus) — tool · protocol — CAN Bus Parser decodes VanMoof SA5 and later bicycle CAN captures from CSV or candump input. It reassembles multi-frame CBOR messages, maps device IDs and heartbeat frames, and provides live or saved-capture diagnostics for components such as the electronic lock and shifter. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/vanmoof-can-bus-parser/))
- [CAN Commander](https://github.com/MatthewKuKanich/CAN_Commander) — tool · protocol — A Flipper Zero app and earlier Arduino sketches for reverse engineering CAN bus traffic, chiefly in vehicles. The current app is a UART front end for a CAN Commander ESP32 board: it streams all or filtered frames, lists unique IDs, measures message rates, tracks per-bit and per-byte changes, and runs a two-phase Auto Reverse Engineer that first calibrates out background traffic and then shows which IDs change during an action. It also decodes live signals from DBC-based profiles and reads OBD-II PIDs, the VIN and trouble codes. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/can-commander/))
- [CanLab](https://github.com/Sherin-SEF-AI/CanLab) — tool · protocol — PyQt6 desktop workstation and command-line tool for reverse-engineering CAN bus captures offline. It reads SavvyCAN/GVRET CSV, candump, pcap, Vector BLF/ASC, MDF4 and openpilot logs, then suggests counters, checksums and their algorithms, byte roles, flags, multiplexers, entropy-based field boundaries and cross-ID correlations, decodes J1939 and NMEA 2000 PGNs and reassembles multi-frame messages. A cantools-backed DBC builder checks signal definitions against the captured frames and exports DBC, openpilot DBC, CANdb++, ARXML and Wireshark Lua dissectors, and an optional MCP server offers 29 receive-only analysis tools to assistants. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/canlab/))
- [cantools](https://github.com/cantools/cantools) — tool · protocol — Python 3 library and command-line tool for CAN bus databases that parses DBC, KCD, SYM, ARXML 3/4 and CDD files and encodes and decodes CAN messages, including simple and extended signal multiplexing and diagnostic DIDs. Its CLI decodes candump output against a database, plots decoded signals from candump logs with matplotlib, dumps each message's bit layout, converts between database formats, generates C pack and unpack code, and monitors a live bus in a text interface through python-can. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/cantools/))
- [Caring Caribou](https://github.com/CaringCaribou/caringcaribou) — tool · protocol — Python automotive security exploration tool, begun in the HEAVENS research project, that the README describes as a zero-knowledge tool to drop onto a CAN network and collect information about the services present. Passive modules dump CAN traffic to the terminal or a candump-format file and list every arbitration ID seen on the bus, while the UDS (ISO 14229-1), DoIP (ISO 13400-2) and XCP modules discover ECUs that answer diagnostic requests, enumerate their supported services and subservices, read data identifiers and query XCP capabilities. Its utilities provide reusable ISO-TP (ISO 15765-2) and UDS implementations, and new modules plug into a drop-in module architecture. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/caringcaribou/))
- [CNNPRE (IEEE Access 2023)](https://github.com/JG91/CNNPRE) — research · protocol — Research code and data for CNNPRE, a message-type identification method for protocol reverse engineering by Javad Garshasbi and Mehdi Teimouri, published in IEEE Access in 2023. A single Colab notebook turns each message's padded bit string into a resized grayscale image, extracts features with an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet50 as transfer learning, adds message direction and length-group encodings, reduces the features with UMAP and clusters messages with agglomerative clustering and HDBSCAN, scoring each clustering against labelled message types. Preprocessed message datasets for twelve protocols, from ARP and DNS to SMTP and GSM, accompany the notebook. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/cnnpre/))
- [Deluder](https://github.com/Warxim/deluder) — tool · app, protocol — Frida-based interceptor for the traffic of proxy-unaware applications. JavaScript hook scripts on the send and receive functions of WinSock, Linux libc sockets, OpenSSL, GnuTLS and SChannel pass plaintext buffers to Python interceptor modules, which log them or forward them to a proxy for inspection. It spawns or attaches to a process, optionally through a remote frida-server, and was designed mainly as the backend for the author's PETEP proxy; a proxifier interceptor works with other proxy tools, and new scripts and interceptors can be added. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/deluder/))
- [eCapture](https://github.com/gojue/ecapture) — tool · app, protocol — eBPF-based Linux and Android tool that attaches uprobes to TLS libraries such as OpenSSL, BoringSSL, GnuTLS and NSS, and to Go's TLS stack, recording the plaintext a process sends and receives without a proxy or an installed CA certificate, so analysts can inspect an application's encrypted traffic on a host they control. Output modes write plaintext text, NSS key logs, or pcapng files with embedded TLS secrets for Wireshark and tshark, and a text mode can rebuild complete HTTP/1.1 and HTTP/2 messages from the captured stream. Separate modules audit bash and zsh commands and MySQL and PostgreSQL queries. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/ecapture/))
- [Facedancer](https://github.com/greatscottgadgets/facedancer) — tool · protocol — Python library for implementing USB devices in software, with a hardware peripheral such as Cynthion or GreatFET acting as a remote-controlled USB controller attached to the target host. Its USBProxy mode sits between a real device and a host, forwarding transfers while filters log, decode or modify them for USB protocol analysis; the README notes this suits devices whose software you do not control, such as embedded systems or game consoles. Version 3 is a ground-up rewrite of the emulation core that ships keyboard, mass-storage and FTDI serial device models plus HID and logging proxy filters, and no longer runs legacy 2.9.x scripts. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/facedancer/))
- [FISSURE](https://github.com/ainfosec/FISSURE) — tool · protocol — GNU Radio-based RF framework that carries a signal from survey and detection through IQ capture, inspection, conditioning, feature extraction and classification into a protocol discovery workflow. Its demodulation workspace estimates symbol rates for OOK/ASK and binary FSK recordings, and the bitstream and framing stage applies Manchester or differential decoding, sync-pattern and CRC evidence and repeated-message detection to propose message boundaries. A Qt dashboard coordinates local or remote SDR sensor nodes, a library keeps protocol and packet definitions, dissectors and archived recordings, and plugins add inspection modules such as radar pulse analysis. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/fissure/))
- [friTap](https://github.com/fkie-cad/friTap) — tool · app, protocol — Frida-based tool that hooks TLS libraries such as OpenSSL, BoringSSL, NSS and GnuTLS inside a running process and writes NSS key logs and decrypted payloads as PCAP, so analysts can inspect an application's encrypted traffic in Wireshark. For stripped or statically linked libraries it accepts byte patterns or offsets and can scan for renamed libraries, or it can recover TLS secrets by scanning heap memory instead of hooking. A terminal UI, a Python API and a Wireshark extcap integration wrap the same capture. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/fritap/))
- [furiousMAC Continuity](https://github.com/furiousMAC/continuity) — tool · protocol — Reverse-engineering notes and a Wireshark dissector for Apple's proprietary Continuity Bluetooth LE advertisements, continuing the authors' PETS 2019 privacy study. Per-message pages document field layouts and observed values for 14 message types, including AirDrop, Handoff, Proximity Pairing, Nearby Info and Find My, and the dissector replaces Wireshark's packet-bthci_cmd.c so captured advertisements decode into named btcommon.apple fields. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/furiousmac-continuity/))
- [gr-lora](https://github.com/rpp0/gr-lora) — tool · protocol — GNU Radio out-of-tree module by Pieter Robyns and co-authors for receiving and decoding LoRa physical-layer frames with a software-defined radio, implementing the modulation features described in patents and blog posts. Its receiver channelizes the input, detects and synchronizes to LoRa chirps with clock-drift correction, and decodes symbols through Gray mapping, deinterleaving, dewhitening and Hamming decoding into header and payload bytes, which a message socket sink can forward over UDP port 40868. The bundled flowgraphs decode recorded traces or live captures, and the README lists HackRF One, USRP B201, RTL-SDR and LimeSDR as tested receivers. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/gr-lora/))
- [grpc-tools](https://github.com/bradleyjkemp/grpc-tools) — tool · app, protocol — Bradley Kemp's suite of gRPC debugging tools, described as Fiddler or Charles for gRPC. grpc-dump runs as a local HTTP(S) proxy that logs every gRPC and gRPC-Web call an application makes as a JSON stream with service and method names, metadata, any error status and each message both raw and parsed; without the .proto files it decodes messages heuristically. grpc-replay re-sends the recorded requests and checks the server's responses, grpc-fixture answers clients from a saved dump, and the grpc-proxy Go library underlies them. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/grpc-tools/))
- [Hermes](https://github.com/at0m-b0mb/Hermes-FlipperZero) — tool · protocol — Hermes is a Flipper Zero app for identifying unknown UART settings and inspecting a device's serial console. It timestamps line edges to estimate bit time, checks candidate rates and parity against received UART bytes, then offers a terminal with text, hex and capture features. The source states that detection leaves the transmit pin undriven until the user sends data. ([entry](https://awesomerea.org/resources/hermes-flipperzero/))
- [Holley EFI Parser](https://github.com/HackingLZ/holley-efi-parser) — tool · protocol — Holley EFI Parser decodes Holley DL and DLZ data logs and live ECU CAN messages.
